   338	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   339	// cinatra#2591 deliverable 4 — DETERMINISTIC row recovery.
   340	//
   341	// The projector seeds one anchor entity node per projected row, with a UUID it
   342	// chooses, and persists the uuid the server RESOLVED. Recall inverts that map
   343	// (`objects.graphiti_anchor_node_uuid`) instead of hoping the extraction model
   344	// emitted the row id somewhere.
   345	//
   346	// The mechanism is proven against a REAL store in
   347	// `scripts/ci/works-after/rt/graphiti-roundtrip.ts`; these tests pin the
   348	// projector's half of the contract.
   349	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   350	describe("deterministic anchor seeding (cinatra#2591)", () => {
   351	  it("seeds a lane-scoped anchor whose uuid is a pure function of the row + lane", async () => {
   352	    runPg.mockReturnValueOnce([{ rows: [memoryRow()] }]);
   353	    runPg.mockReturnValue([{ rows: [], rowCount: 1 }]);
   354	    const result = await projectObjectToGraphiti({
   355	      objectId: "obj-mem-1",
   356	      objectVersion: 1,
   357	      orgId: ORG,
   358	    });
   359	
   360	    expect(addTrip).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
   361	    const seed = addTrip.mock.calls[0][0];
   362	    // Same lane as the episode — an anchor in the wrong lane would be invisible
   363	    // to a recall the caller IS entitled to.
   364	    expect(seed.group_id).toBe(`${BASE}-user-user-42`);
   365	    // The anchor's NAME is the row's entity name, which is what graphiti embeds
   366	    // and what NODE_HYBRID_SEARCH_RRF ranks on — this is why the anchor is
   367	    // findable by a semantic query rather than being an inert marker.
   368	    expect(seed.source_node_name).toBe("No default exports");
   369	    // Deterministic uuid, distinct from the episode uuid (separate namespace).
   370	    expect(seed.source_node_uuid).toBe(anchorNodeUuidFor("obj-mem-1", `${BASE}-user-user-42`));
   371	    expect(seed.source_node_uuid).not.toBe(`obj-mem-1@${BASE}-user-user-42`);
   372	    expect(result.anchorNodeUuid).toBe(seed.source_node_uuid);
   373	  });
   374	
   375	  it("persists the uuid the SERVER resolved, not the one it proposed", async () => {
   376	    // graphiti may merge a new node onto an existing near-duplicate. Recall will
   377	    // see the survivor's uuid, so that is what the column must hold.
   378	    addTrip.mockResolvedValueOnce({
   379	      message: "ok",
   380	      nodes: [{ uuid: "merged-onto-existing", name: "No default exports" }],
   381	      edges: [],
   382	    });
   383	    runPg.mockReturnValueOnce([{ rows: [memoryRow()] }]);
   384	    runPg.mockReturnValue([{ rows: [], rowCount: 1 }]);
   385	    await projectObjectToGraphiti({ objectId: "obj-mem-1", objectVersion: 1, orgId: ORG });
   386	
   387	    const markCall = runPg.mock.calls.find((c) =>
   388	      /graphiti_anchor_node_uuid\s*=\s*COALESCE/i.test(c[0]?.queries?.[0]?.text ?? ""),
   389	    );
   390	    expect(markCall).toBeDefined();
   391	    expect(markCall![0].queries[0].values).toContain("merged-onto-existing");
   392	  });
   393	
   394	  it("a failed seed does NOT fail the projection — the episode still counts", async () => {
   395	    // The episode is already handed over (and already billed). Failing here
   396	    // would make the outbox retry forever and re-bill extraction every cycle.
   397	    addTrip.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("graphiti refused the triplet"));
   398	    runPg.mockReturnValueOnce([{ rows: [memoryRow()] }]);
   399	    runPg.mockReturnValue([{ rows: [], rowCount: 1 }]);
   400	    const result = await projectObjectToGraphiti({
   401	      objectId: "obj-mem-1",
   402	      objectVersion: 1,
   403	      orgId: ORG,
   404	    });
   405	
   406	    expect(result.skipped).toBeUndefined();
   407	    expect(result.anchorNodeUuid).toBeNull();
   408	    expect(addEp).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
   409	    // NULL is passed, and COALESCE in the UPDATE keeps any previously-good
   410	    // anchor rather than clearing it on one transient failure.
   411	    const markCall = runPg.mock.calls.find((c) =>
   412	      /graphiti_anchor_node_uuid\s*=\s*COALESCE/i.test(c[0]?.queries?.[0]?.text ?? ""),
   413	    );
   414	    expect(markCall![0].queries[0].values).toContain(null);
   415	  });
   416	
   417	  it("the anchor uuid is stable across re-projections and distinct per lane", async () => {
   418	    // Stability is what makes the seed an UPSERT rather than a duplicate; lane
   419	    // distinctness is what keeps a lane-move from stranding the old anchor.
   420	    const userLane = `${BASE}-user-user-42`;
   421	    const teamLane = `${BASE}-team-team-9`;
   422	    expect(anchorNodeUuidFor("obj-mem-1", userLane)).toBe(anchorNodeUuidFor("obj-mem-1", userLane));
   423	    expect(anchorNodeUuidFor("obj-mem-1", userLane)).not.toBe(anchorNodeUuidFor("obj-mem-1", teamLane));
   424	    expect(anchorNodeUuidFor("obj-mem-1", userLane)).not.toBe(anchorNodeUuidFor("obj-mem-2", userLane));
   425	  });
   426	});

codex
- **BLOCKER — key-free mode still sends row content to OpenAI.** `scripts/gen-graphiti-env.mjs:170-177`, `docker/graphiti/config.yaml:34-43`, `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:905-912`. The sentinel constructs a real OpenAI LLM client against `api.openai.com`; every keyless `add_memory` transmits the episode body with an invalid credential before receiving 401. This violates the vendor-free/no-provider state and can exfiltrate object content.

- **HIGH — fresh `docker compose up` crash-loops Graphiti.** `docker-compose.yml:568-570`, `docker/graphiti/config.yaml:34-63`. The generated env file is optional, but without it both configured OpenAI clients lack credentials—the exact startup crash described in the empirical context. Direct Compose startup or generator failure never reaches healthy state.

- **BLOCKER — anchors do not recover rows from ordinary graph hits.** `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:451-460`, `packages/objects/src/mcp/handlers.ts:509-518`. Only the title/name anchor UUID maps to a row. A query matching episode body content ranks an extracted entity UUID, which has no anchor-column mapping, so recovery falls back to the known-inert probes and loses the row. Example: title “Q3 notes,” body “Contoso acquired Northwind,” query “Northwind acquisition.”

- **HIGH — Graphiti merging makes the inverse map lossy.** `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:462-472`, `src/lib/objects-store.ts:640-644`. Two same-lane rows with identical/near-identical names may resolve to one node UUID. Both rows store that UUID, but `Map<string,string>` retains only whichever unordered SQL row is processed last; the other row becomes unrecoverable.

- **HIGH — transient anchor failure is permanent for that version.** `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:473-480`, `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:503-512`, `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:647-655`. Failure is swallowed and the version is marked projected, so the equal-version guard prevents retry. A one-off embedder/MCP outage leaves the row dependent on incidental recovery until another object mutation.

- **HIGH — `COALESCE` corrupts lane-move bookkeeping after seed failure.** `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:509-510`. On a lane move, `projected_group_id` advances to the new lane while the old-lane anchor UUID is retained. The new lane has no searchable anchor, the old lane’s episode is deleted, and the completed version will not retry.

- **HIGH — anchor lifecycle ignores retraction.** `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:541-547`, `packages/objects/src/graphiti-projector.ts:566-580`. Transitioning to public/unprojected or claim-`none` deletes only the episode and leaves both the anchor node and its Postgres mapping. Searches in the former lane can still resolve and return a row whose projection policy now says it must not be indexed.

- **HIGH — Anthropic/Gemini configurations are never selected.** `src/lib/knowledge-graph-indexing.ts:114-157`, `scripts/gen-graphiti-env.mjs:171-191`. Resolution reads only the OpenAI connection and always emits `LLM__PROVIDER=openai`. An Anthropic-only installation is incorrectly classified as extraction-off despite the new server/config supporting Anthropic.

- **HIGH — the “pinned” images are not reproducible.** `docker/graphiti/Dockerfile:68-69,107-112`, `docker/kg-embedder/Dockerfile:14,31-37`. The Graphiti build executes an unpinned remote installer and regenerates `uv.lock` against current transitive releases; the embedder model revision and dependency hashes are also unpinned. The same local image tag can produce different code or vectors on different build dates.

No authorization widening was found in the resolver path: candidates are still intersected with ownership/project filters and per-row authorization. `shouldPreserveExisting` correctly excludes the two sentinel values, and generated Compose keys do not collide with `environment:` keys.
